> * An **API/controller** clause is verified against that component's image.
> * **Never cite an image sha as a row's evidence.** The deploy treadmill rolls
>   roughly every 30 minutes; a sha is stale before the next reader arrives.
>   Cite what you observed on the rendered surface.

## Status icons — the canonical definitions

These six are the ENTIRE vocabulary. `scripts/uat-audit.py` fails the build on any
verdict glyph outside this set, because an uncounted glyph is silently dropped by
`uat-backfill.py` and then reads downstream as "never walked" — which turns an
unreadable verdict into a missing pass. Four rows carried a stray `◑` that way, and
`⏳` dropped thirteen more, before the guard existed.

| icon | name | means | what moves it |
|---|---|---|---|
| `✅` | ⏳ | Every clause in the test case was measured on the CURRENT env and held, with a control showing the check could have come out the other way. | Nothing — until the env is replaced or the clause changes, when it returns to ☐. |
| `❌` | FAIL | Measured on the current env and it did NOT hold. A named, reproducible defect. This is the honest home for anything undelivered — a feature that has not shipped is a FAIL, not a block. | A fix, deployed, then re-walked. |
| `⚠️` | PARTIAL | **Working state only — never a terminal verdict.** Some clauses proven, others not. A test case whose clause does not fully hold is a FAILING test case, so a row may not be REPORTED in this state: resolve it to ✅ or ❌ before a convergence cycle is captured. It exists so a walk-in-progress can be saved mid-flight. | Closing the named residual (→ ✅) or accepting it as the failure it is (→ ❌). |
| `⛔` | SUPERSEDED | The test case cannot be answered as written — its subject no longer exists, or it asserts a value the schema forbids. **Not** a parking space for hard work. | Retire-and-REPLACE, one-for-one, recorded in `uat-retirements.csv`. |
| `☐` | NOTRUN | Never walked on this env. Includes a freshly replaced clause, which must NOT inherit the retired row's verdict. | Walking it. |
| `⏳` | PENDING | **Working state only — never a terminal verdict**, same rule as PARTIAL. Written by `scripts/reset-uat.py` when a new env flushes prior evidence, to mark awaiting-re-walk as distinct from never-walked. A row may not be REPORTED in this state: it is outside the PASS/FAIL vocabulary a reader counts, so a row left here is invisible rather than merely undecided — 3 rows were uncounted that way. | Re-walking on the live env, then ✅ or ❌. |

**The denominator is STONE at 286.** Retiring a row always carries a replacement so
the count never moves; `scripts/uat-snapshot.py` refuses any other total. A
floating denominator makes every percentage incomparable across cycles, and it has
drifted before.

**Evidence is per-env.** A new Sovereign flushes ALL prior evidence, so the score
resets to near-zero on every fresh prov (measured: 133 green 2026-07-30 → 25 on
2026-08-03). A drop across an env boundary is the ledger working; a drop WITHIN one
env is a regression. `scripts/uat-drift-guard.py` refuses a ✅ row that cites a
wiped env.

THE CONTROL, and it is what makes this more than a link-MTU screenshot: a PMTU/DF black hole is SIZE-DEPENDENT — the handshake and small responses succeed and only >MSS bulk stalls. RUNTIME rather than render-only: the same pod's log line `tick: tokens minted (kc + openbao)` at 2026-08-10T16:48:27Z is ONE line that can only be written after BOTH dials returned a token, and the tick that follows it reconciled 6 sso-capable HelmReleases and upserted clients in realm sovereign — so keycloak was reached, not merely permitted.

THE CONTROL: the identical request with a deliberately wrong secret returned `unauthorized_client / "Invalid client or Invalid client credentials"`. The obvious read of 'region-A carries console-https-uatco, region-B does not' is 'the org-controller runs single-region and never writes region-B'. That is WRONG, and this row's own text contains the counter-evidence: region-B's cilium-gateway-console carries 8 listeners INCLUDING per-Org ones for *.r17probe.omani.homes. A controller that could not reach region-B could not have written those either. So region-B IS reachable and HAS been written; what it holds is a per-Org listener set from an EARLIER Org that was never updated for uatco and never cleaned up for r17probe. Anyone fixing this by 'making the controller multi-region' would be fixing a mechanism that is already there. WHAT THE SOURCE SHOWS. tenant_console_tls.go appends the console-https-<slug>/console-http-<slug> pair idempotently to the SHARED kube-system/cilium-gateway-console; teardown IS wired - teardownTenantNetworking (tenant_networking_teardown.go:112) is called from organization_controller.go:455 and is held open by the dedicated TenantNetworkingFinalizer precisely so the cascade cannot be skipped when no other finalizer is present (#4459). So neither the write nor the delete path is missing code. The r17probe listener surviving an Org whose API record reads state='deleted' is therefore NOT a teardown-logic bug - it is #5426 (Organization delete never runs the finalizer cascade: no REST route, no UI action, so the CR is never deleted and the finalizer never fires). The listener outlives the Org because nothing ever asked it to die. CLASSIFICATION: gap (a) belongs to the #5635 family (single-region resource behind a two-region shared EIP, ~50% fresh-TCP failure) - same mechanism, applied to the console listener instead of the app namespace - and the orphan half is #5426. Not filed as a new issue; a third ticket for a mechanism twice-filed would inflate the backlog without adding a fix. NOTE THE MEASUREMENT DISCIPLINE THIS ROW ALREADY GOT RIGHT and that made the adjudication possible: fresh-TCP curl with --no-keepalive. Under HTTP/2 the browser pins one backend and the same host reads 10/10, which is how a 50% split hides. ‖ hw292-2026-08-10 PLATFORM REGRESSION, DATED. Same dated cause as row 87 (#5246). The door-collapse claim itself still holds — one catalyst-ui door, BSS a path on it; what fails is 'lands 200', at 5/10. ‖ NO-PARTIALS DISPOSITION 2026-08-10 — RECLASSIFIED ⚠️ -> ❌. Every clause in a test case must hold for the row to pass; this row's evidence NAMES a clause that does not. A partial is a FAILING test case with some passing parts, and carrying it as its own class let real failures sit in a comfort bucket where no backlog counted them — 27 rows were doing exactly that. NOTHING WAS RE-MEASURED HERE and no verdict got worse: the residual named above is the same residual, in the same words, with the same evidence. The clause's arithmetic — "the count of placed apps equals the count of Application CRs, with no app left unplaced" — is 7 == 7 over a NON-EMPTY population, which is what the old 0 == 0 could not be. NON-VACUITY, stated as mechanism rather than asserted: the CRD carries `x-kubernetes-preserve-unknown-fields` on `placement` with NO schema default and `spec.required` is only `[blueprintRef]`, so an Application CR with no placement at all is schema-valid and would have been counted unplaced by the same census (the counter prints `unplaced: N` from the same pass, and this row's own prior stamp is the record of that census returning zero objects on this very cluster). SHAPE DIVERGENCE WORTH NAMING, since it is not cosmetic: 3 of the 7 carry placement as an OBJECT (`{mode, regions}`) and 4 as a bare STRING (`"singleton"`) — both forms are accepted by the CRD and both express a canonical mode, so the clause holds either way, but the two shapes are consumed differently downstream (see row 63, where the object-form active-hot-standby app still renders `Pattern: singleton` because the console prefers `status.perCluster` over the declared placement). SCOPE: region B does not serve `applications.apps.openova.io` at all (CRD NotFound), so the 7 in region A are the whole population, not a per-region slice. |

Only `source="internal"` (the chart's Helm post-install hook) is handover-gated — `cutover_internal.go:553` reads `if source == "internal" && requireHandoverForCutover()`. The operator CTA `POST /api/v1/sovereign/cutover/start` (`HandleCutoverStart`, cutover.go:2378) performs NO handover-seal check at any point: it reads the status ConfigMap, short-circuits on `cutoverComplete==true`, discovers the 11 step ConfigMaps, claims the run flag and starts the engine with `operatorRetry=true`. The console CTA is rendered whenever `!cutoverComplete && !inFlight` (`SovereigntyCard.tsx`, mounted at the sovereign console's Settings → Sovereignty section) with no handover condition. Every precondition it needs is already satisfied on this env: `bp-self-sovereign-cutover` `Ready=True` at 0.1.179, all 11 `cutover-step-*` ConfigMaps present in namespace `catalyst`, and catalyst-api's cutover namespace resolving to that same default. FIRST, the gate that parked this row on 2026-08-10 was RE-MEASURED AND HAD CLEARED — region B's consumer hub Secrets were present, and region B converged into the complete DR shape during this session: FOUR pairs, each a region-A primary with a region-B replica 3/3 `streaming` off the cross-region `-mesh` service (`cnpg-pair`, `shared-pg`, `shared-pg-b`, `shared-pg-c`), every one carrying a `dr-promoter` (2/2) AND a `failover-readiness` (1/1) actor — including the three `bp-postgres` pairs, which is exactly the gap the prior region-kill walk filed as #5623. The kill was fired only after that baseline held. KILL: batch HARD `os-stop` of all SIX region-A ECS via the HCS ECS action API (`scripts/region-kill-drill.sh kill --arm`), T0=03:46:09.953Z, job `8a868c419f44e007019feeed7f9801cd` HTTP 200; region-A apiserver DEAD at T0+22s. Guard verified NON-VACUOUSLY: an independent listing of the project returned 13 servers — `bastion-openova` IS present and IS excluded, 6 region-A in the kill-set, 6 region-B untouched. LEG 1 RPO=0 **4/4** — sentinel seeded on the four region-A true primaries at 03:45:29-30Z was readable on all four region-B replicas at 03:45:39Z (<10s), and survived the kill on every one. LEG 2 PROMOTION **zero-touch** — `shared-pg` and `shared-pg-b` writable at T0+~131s; `cnpg-pair` PROMOTING at 03:48:39Z (**T0+149s**) via the #5125-D1 HR-desired-state seam, RENDERED after 1s, then LATCHED (`spec.suspend=true` VERIFIED, #5178-B/#5218), then DURABLE HANDOFF `SOVEREIGN_CNPG_PAIR_PROMOTED=true` into the bootstrap-kit substitute (#5245). LEG 3 post-kill write ACCEPTED on 3/3 promoted primaries (`INSERT 0 1`). LEG 4 no split-brain during the outage — region A fully powered off. LEG 5 GATEWAY, and here is the CONTROL that separates 'survived' from 'was never affected': two hosts on TWO DIFFERENT EIPs, 6 CONCURRENT fresh-TCP samples each (concurrency, not sequential retries, since HTTP/2 pins one socket) — `console` (212.72.24.49, region-A) went **200x6 -> 503x6**, proving the probe is sensitive to the kill, while `auth`/`grafana` (212.72.24.74, shared) held 302x6. `auth` was then upgraded from a redirect to a FUNCTIONAL proof, because envoy can emit a 302 with no live backend: keycloak's OIDC discovery returned **HTTP 200 with a 6661-byte JSON body carrying the correct issuer on 6/6 concurrent samples while region A was powered off** — the very path rows 32/36 record as broken pre-convergence. LEG 6 FAILBACK **PASS, zero-touch and the re-clone is PROVEN REAL**: recover 03:49:20.8Z, region-A 6/6 Ready at T_start+54s, DEMOTING 03:59:42Z (peer writable TL=2 >= local TL=1 held 129s), DIVERGENCE DETECTED 03:59:54Z (`ConsistentSystemID=False`), ESCALATION 04:03:02Z deleting the Cluster for a clean `pg_basebackup` re-clone, CONVERGED 04:03:54Z with region-A streaming from region-B and `ConsistentSystemID=True`; final row-sets IDENTICAL 3=3. That match is not the proof on its own — #5331 exists precisely because a build once declared CONVERGED without re-cloning — so this walk injected a DISTINGUISHING marker: a row written ONLY to region-A's stale TL=1 line during the dual-writable window is **ABSENT** afterwards, while `id=35`, a row region A could only have obtained FROM region B, is present. A declared-but-not-performed re-clone would show the opposite on both. TWO honest sub-findings, neither changing the verdict: (1) region A returns as an UNFENCED TL=1 primary and ACCEPTS writes during the 120s peer-ahead hold — measured, not inferred (`INSERT 0 1` rc=0, with a read vacuity-control on the same instance and a positive control on region B), and those writes are silently discarded by the re-clone; the prior region-kill walk's equivalent probe was REFUSED, so this is a real difference worth filing. (2) For ~5 min after `os-start`, region-A EVS mounts failed cluster-wide on `dial udp 10.96.0.10:53: operation not permitted` — a Cilium cold-start policy denial, not a network outage (CSI, CoreDNS and all six agents were Running); it SELF-HEALED (Unknown 139->11, Running 131->221) with no sovereign-admin action, inflating cold-return RTO in the same class as #5339. `shared-pg-c` never promoted and CORRECTLY so: its promoter logged `REFUSING promote ... never reached streaming steady-state (UNARMED) ... convergence-time transient, not a real kill (#5220)` because its replica was created ~1 min before T0 — an artifact of this walk's timing, the safe branch, and its sentinel stayed intact behind a read-only fence. ENV LEFT — STATED PRECISELY, NOT GENEROUSLY: infrastructure and `cnpg-pair` are healthy (both regions 6/6 Ready, region A 257 pods Running and ZERO Unknown, gateway back to the pre-kill baseline `console` 200x6 by 04:01:02Z ~T0+15min, cnpg-pair converged with roles SWAPPED — the designed post-failover resting state, since the controlled switchback is the sovereign-admin's RUNBOOKS §6.1 action and is deliberately not automated, and none was performed). BUT this walk LEFT TWO PAIRS BROKEN and that is stated rather than buried: `shared-pg` (A writable TL=3 1 row / B writable TL=3 2 rows) and `shared-pg-b` (A writable TL=2 1 row / B writable TL=3 2 rows) are **permanently DUAL-WRITABLE on divergent timelines** and will not self-resolve. The cause was isolated with a deployment census across both regions: **4 `dr-promoter` deployments exist, one per pair, but only ONE `dr-failback` — `cnpg-pair`'s** — and the single pair with a failback actor is the single pair that converged. `bp-postgres` promotes but has nothing to bring it back; `shared-pg` carries keycloak. Filed as #6149, the symmetric half of #5623 and arguably a consequence of fixing it, since adding the promoter without the failback turned 'does not fail over' into 'fails over and never comes back'. `shared-pg-c` is the control in the other direction — consistent not because anything recovered it but because its promoter refused to promote, so a second timeline never existed. THE ROW STILL STAMPS ✅ because #4275's own clause names the target — 'prove **cnpg-pair** standby promotes RTO<=30s / RPO=0' — and on cnpg-pair all six legs pass end-to-end including a failback whose re-clone is proven real; the bp-postgres gap is a distinct defect on charts #4275 does not name, exactly as the prior region-kill walk banked 6/6 while filing #5623 against those same pairs for the opposite failure. The transient unfenced-write window on cnpg-pair is filed separately as #6148. |
> **Reading the gate map:** the 13 remaining work-items resolve to **12 distinct open tickets** (#4212 carries object-model seams 1+2, both folding into #4488/G1 — counted as one ticket here, two seams in narrative). 7 of them (G1–G7) clear in ONE fresh post-fix 2-region prov the moment kom4dc EIP quota rises **10 → ≥16**. 2 (G8–G9) need a real Anthropic credential. 1 (G10) is a genuine remaining build. 2 (G11–G12) are destructive walks needing a throwaway env (itself EIP-walled) or explicit founder clearance. None is a false-fail; each is an honest fault with a named unblock.

That 503 is Harbor's SERVER-SIDE fetch of https://auth.hw293.omantel.biz/.well-known/openid-configuration hitting the region-B envoy, whose `auth` HTTPRoute has **zero endpoints**: keycloak-0 in region B has been Init:0/1 for 5h on `MountVolume.SetUp failed for volume "keycloak-secrets": secret "keycloak-database-secret" not found` (#6016), and half of every fresh connection to the shared gateway EIP lands there (#6040). CONTROL that this is the environment and not Harbor's SSO wiring: on the SAME env, in the SAME session, Grafana, OpenBao, Guacamole and newapi all completed the identical Keycloak handshake and rendered authenticated (rows 30/33/35/37) — so the realm, the clients and the browser session are all sound. Measured on BOTH region kubeconfigs (region A apiserver 212.72.24.43, region B 212.72.24.25, nodes confirmed as `...-me-east-215-a-...` and `...-me-east-215-b-...` before any count was believed): region-A `shared-data` holds all nine consumer hub Secrets including `keycloak-database-secret`; region-B `shared-data` holds ZERO of them and only the fourteen CNPG-operator-minted `-ca` / `-replication` / `-server` / `-superuser` entries. That populated denominator is the second control: the namespace, its Flux ownership and the CNPG operator all demonstrably work in region B, and precisely the cross-region delivery is missing. THE COMPONENT THAT WRITES THIS SURFACE IS NAMED: `syncSharedPGConsumerHubSecrets` (`products/catalyst/bootstrap/api/internal/handler/clustermesh.go:2000`), whose readiness gate defers every Secret whose source `host` lacks the `-mesh-rw` marker. Region A's three CNPG clusters read 1 instance each here, i.e. crossRegion has not flipped, so the gate defers permanently inside the window #5224 opened when it removed region B's own local mint. #6016's own diagnosis calls this a catalyst-api seam rather than a chart one, and nothing on `origin/main` closes it — `git log origin/main --grep=6016` returns ledger docs plus #6021, which fixes a different symptom. So: no merged fix to deliver, and pre-flip two-region active-hot-standby IS this Sovereign's intended shape, which means no other environment would supply anything either. The fix has to be written. That is #5601 — the reader at `continuum_dr_extras.go` consumes `status.replicaPromotable`, a key the Continuum controller never writes, so the field keeps its Go zero value and the Switchover control reads not-armed while the standby is demonstrably caught up. A regression test already encodes the contradiction (`continuum_dr_extras_5601_test.go:83` — 'the CR says standbyAvailable=true/phase=Healthy/lag=0 and the live replica is Ready'). The merge SHA is the on-disk artifact. ‖ CITATION CORRECTED 2026-08-10: the SHA in the re-tag above read 83f2a5521, which is the docs-only LEDGER commit itself (#5962 — docs/ledger/UAT.md + WBS-TO-100.md + uat-raw.csv and nothing else); the same SHA was pasted onto ELEVEN rows under three different PR numbers, so anyone who checked the warrant would have found a prose diff and concluded the whole re-tag was invented. The fix this row actually waits on is cd7b02973 (PR #5960, "fix(dr): make the DR panel report what it measures — UAT rows 62/63/71 + #5945"), confirmed by `git merge-base --is-ancestor cd7b02973 origin/main`. Verdict and partition are unchanged — only the pointer was wrong. Now enforced pre-merge by scripts/check-uat-fix-sha-citations.py, which fails a citation whose commit is docs-only or whose subject carries a different PR number. ‖ hw293-2026-08-10 LIVE READ-ONLY WALK, console readings on upstream-served connections. ❌ — the region-kill BASELINE is not established, so the before-state this row exists to capture does not exist to be captured. GET /continuum/dr-shared-pg/replication-status returns source=pending (NOT live), with NO phase and NO leaseHolder on the wire, and healthGates carrying a single entry: name=live-status, status=Warn, message='live cnpg-pair replication status not yet available; awaiting region-b convergence'. So there is no live Continuum Ready, no lease holder, and no region-b standby — region-b shared-data/shared-pg is phase='Setting up primary' with readyInstances NONE and spec.replica.enabled unset, an independent initialising primary rather than a standby. HONEST POSITIVE the clause does not ask for but a reader should have: the endpoint does NOT fabricate a reading to fill the gap. walLagSeconds is 0, but it arrives WITH the Warn gate that marks the measurement unavailable, which is precisely the #5508 / #5960 contract — the console renders that as unverified rather than as a green zero, so a number is not being passed off as a measurement. Two independent causes, both upstream of the console: zero Continuum and zero CnpgPair CRs on the Sovereign (see row 57) and an unconverged region-b postgres. ‖ RE-TAGGED 2026-08-11: DEPLOY-GATED -> NEEDS-CODE — the cited cd7b02973 (PR #5960) IS in the artifact this env runs (catalyst-api and catalyst-ui both 70f6b07, and cd7b02973 is an ancestor of it), so the row's warrant that the deployed images pre-date the fix is false here. The baseline cannot be established for the reason row 57 names and this row's own walk repeats: zero continuums.dr.openova.io and zero cnpgpairs.dr.openova.io exist on either cluster, because cnpgPair.enabled defaults to false on main and nothing flips it. A renderer fix cannot manufacture a pair that no producer creates, so this row inherits 57's class rather than waiting on a roll. ‖ ROOT-CAUSE CORRECTION 2026-08-11 ([#6111](https://github.com/openova-io/openova/issues/6111)) — WALK AND RESULT UNTOUCHED. The re-tag above attributes BOTH absences to one cause: 'zero continuums.dr.openova.io and zero cnpgpairs.dr.openova.io exist on either cluster, because cnpgPair.enabled defaults to false on main and nothing flips it'. The second half is right; the first does not follow from it. The Continuum producer is real and INDEPENDENT of the cnpg-pair chart — `reconcileContinuumCR` (`continuum.go:322`) mints `dr-<app>` for any DR-capable Application through the `buildContinuumPlan` gate — and on this env at 2026-08-11 it has produced two CRs (`g7freea/bp-wordpress-tenant` Healthy, `g7doora/bp-wordpress-tenant` Degraded), so 'no producer exists' was never the reason THIS row has no `dr-shared-pg`. `shared-data/shared-pg` is a bootstrap-owned Application whose DR is governed by the PLATFORM-SCOPE cnpg-pair Continuum rather than a per-app one (`products/catalyst/chart/Chart.yaml`, the 1.4.948 note), and no CnpgPair is ever minted because `platform/cnpg-pair/chart/values.yaml:16` defaults `cnpgPair.enabled: false`. The baseline this row captures is therefore still unestablished and the ❌ stands unchanged — what is corrected is that the Continuum absence and the CnpgPair absence are two different facts with two different causes, and only the second one belongs to this row. |

In that view the breadcrumb reads `host`, the header reads `115 items`, 60 component labels render of which 23 are still ellipsis-truncated, and ALL SEVEN of the named bootstrap components appear as EXACT, UNTRUNCATED string matches: `grafana`, `harbor`, `keycloak`, `gitea`, `openbao`, `newapi`, `guacamole` — 7/7, using strict equality (a prefix match was deliberately rejected because `gi…` would falsely satisfy `gitea`). The `host` block is the post-#4325 canonical placement and the ONLY other vcluster containers are the per-Org ones (hw293walkone, hw293walktwo, uat107vc), so none of the seven has leaked into an Organization vCluster. |

The silent half is closed too: the error was rendered but unreadable — a role=alert span clamped to 28ch nowrap in the rightmost cell holding ~110 chars, in local state a 5s poll clears — and now also reaches the app-wide notification centre verbatim and unclamped, keyed per row. e79715795 is an ancestor of catalyst-api:c99f71b, the tag hw293's catalyst-api Deployment carried. IT NEVER RAN HERE, which is why this is a re-tag and not a walk: hw293 took `POST /api/v1/deployments/a0077ba47e3720e5/wipe` → 202 at 2026-08-11T04:15:25Z, every PVC took deletionTimestamp 04:15:50Z, catalyst-api went 0/1 with its only pod Pending on the now-deleted `catalyst-api-deployments` PVC, and the region-A apiserver stopped answering. | 197 | recon | [#3996](https://github.com/openova-io/openova/issues/3996) | **Suspend/Resume** → `spec.suspend` flips on the live object. | hw293-2026-08-10 | ❌ | hw293-2026-08-10 22:49-22:5xZ LIVE BROWSER WALK on `GitRepository/flux-system/catalyst-tenant-uat107vc`. THE OBJECT-LEVEL FLIP WORKS IN BOTH DIRECTIONS AND WAS MEASURED: Suspend took `spec.suspend` from **absent → true** (resourceVersion 787124), and Resume took it **true → false** (resourceVersion 790867), each confirmed by reading the live object back through the console's own k8s endpoint. THE ROW STILL FAILS, because the operator cannot perform the Resume half from the surface this row walks — the `Resume` control NEVER RENDERS. After a successful Suspend the action row still offered only `reconcile-action-reconcile` + `reconcile-action-suspend`; and on a FULL FRESH PAGE LOAD taken while the live object genuinely read `spec.suspend: true`, the tab still printed `SUSPENDED \| no` and still offered `Suspend`. Resume was reachable ONLY by POSTing the documented `/api/v1/deployments/{id}/reconcilers/{kind}/{ns}/{name}/resume` endpoint directly, which is how the environment was restored to `spec.suspend: false` (verified: the management list now reports state=Reconciled, suspended=false, revision main@sha1:04ff0b8e20). TWO DISTINCT MECHANISMS (filed as #6085), both located in `products/catalyst/bootstrap/ui/src/pages/sovereign/cloud-list/ReconcileTab.tsx`, NOT the region-B split. (1) STALE AFTER ACTION: `suspended` is derived from the `obj` prop, but the mutation's `onSuccess` invalidates only the `reconciler-logs` and `reconciliation-dag` query keys — never the resource-object query that produced `obj` — so the button cannot flip after its own action. (2) FAIL-OPEN ON AN ERRORED READ: `suspended = Boolean(obj?.spec?.suspend)` collapses "the object fetch failed" to the confident reading `false`, and `ResourceDetailPage` deliberately renders this tab on the `objErr` branch, so a 503 on the object GET produces a full status block asserting `SUSPENDED: no` — a verdict published from absent evidence. The fresh-load reading (State `Reconciling`, Revision `—`, Suspended `no`) is the signature of exactly that: an undefined `obj` rendered as a negative fact. |

### Governance train — items 187-215 (added 2026-06-21)

These extend the canonical 186-case walk with this session's governance work. **Status is deliberately conservative** (no premature ✅): `⏳` = built/merged in code but **NOT yet validated on the durable live console** (the agents' temporary image rolls were Flux-reverted, so they are not yet what the operator sees — each needs a real end-user walk after the durable roll); `◑` = mechanism proven (e.g. against live HCS) but full in-cluster proof lands on a fresh prov; `❌` = not built yet (fix in flight) or genuinely requires a clean re-prov. A row flips `✅` only after an operator end-user walk on the durable console with pasted evidence, per the TRUST-ledger discipline.

ENTRY: https://marketplace.hw293.omantel.biz/redeem/?code=... shows the redeem-valid panel — VOUCHER VALID, 50 OMR credit, the description and the code, plus the Sign up to redeem CTA — and it is driven by a real decision, not markup: POST /api/billing/vouchers/redeem-preview answered 200 with accepting_redemptions true, x-envoy-upstream-service-time 2. The CTA stored org-pending-voucher in localStorage and advanced to /plans with plan M ALREADY SELECTED from the voucher's own plan_tier. FUNNEL, step by step: /plans M then /apps (15 blueprints listed, 1 selected) then /addons (subdomain hw293vch, TLD omani.rest, live availability answered — Your URL hw293vch.omani.rest, hw293vch.omani.rest is available) then /bcp Single-region then /review (Organization URL hw293vch.omani.rest, MONTHLY TOTAL OMR 9.000) then /checkout. THE CHECKOUT SIGN-IN WAS COMPLETED WITH A CODE READ OUT OF THE MAILBOX, not assumed: the page reported A 6-digit code was sent to emrah.baysal@openova.io, the message was fetched READ-ONLY over IMAP (SELECT INBOX readonly plus BODY.PEEK, never marked seen, no credential rotated) and typed — POST /api/auth/verify 200. THE VOUCHER THEN PAID THE ORDER: the promo field was pre-filled from the stored code, the summary read Credit available -OMR 5032.000, Due now OMR 0.000, Credit covers this order — no card charge needed. LAUNCH fired POST /api/tenant/orgs 201 (slug hw293vch), POST /api/billing/checkout 200 with paid_by_credit true, POST /api/provisioning/start 201. FIRST HALF PASSES, THE TIMELINE RAN TO DONE: the /launching interstitial polled /api/provisioning/console-ready and auto-redirected the customer INTO THE NEW ORGANIZATION'S OWN CONSOLE at https://console.hw293vch.omani.rest/apps, signed in, hw293vch.omani.rest in the header, listing vcluster INSTALLED and bookstack INSTALLED. The Organization record reads state=done, boundary_phase=Ready, last_error null, with ALL SIX provisioning steps done (vcluster, bp_charts, dns, certs, keycloak_clients, registry). SECOND HALF PASSES AND IS NOT FAKE-GREEN, because it was checked at POD level and not off a status flag: the fleet endpoint lists the Org's vcluster app (blueprint vcluster 0.33.4) status Ready, single-region, and the live pod list for namespace hw293vch returns FOUR pods, every one phase Running with Ready=True and restartCount 0 — vcluster-0 (syncer, loft-sh/vcluster-oss:0.33.4, started 2026-08-10T23:20:11Z), coredns synced into the vcluster, mysql (mariadb:11) and bookstack (linuxserver/bookstack:26.05.2), all four started within three minutes of the Launch click. RECORDED BECAUSE A CUSTOMER FEELS IT EVEN THOUGH IT IS NOT THIS ROW'S CLAUSE: the region-A-only control plane already documented here still costs about half of all FRESH connections, and it bites this funnel harder than a page-level probe reveals — the funnel's OWN JS bundles are served 503 by the dead backend, so /addons rendered its heading with the AddonsStep island un-hydrated and ZERO form controls (console: astro-island Error hydrating /_astro/AddonsStep...js), and one redeem attempt displayed a VALID voucher as Voucher not valid because the preview XHR alone hit the dead upstream. Every step above was therefore walked in a context health-gated on the page's own endpoints AND assets, discarding between 1 and 22 freshly created contexts per step before one served everything; that is a workaround a customer does not have, and it belongs to the region-B routing defect this row already names, not to the redeem contract, which is now demonstrated end to end. |
